# Frankfurt run 23 — dip bot run report

*Live 12 h: M2 gate (m2_v3_reg4_top25) + live pre-send (k 4, 20/s, 100-fill cap, 0.1 SOL fee budget) + routes v13 (adds 0slot, skips Rakurai) on dip19; 0.40 SOL, dev list 0.40*

> **Verdict:** +0.652 SOL on the wallet (2.3385 → 2.9904). Reactive M2: 152 buys, 66 filled (50% wins), +0.796 SOL after every cost. Pre-send live: 100 fills (35 wins), realized −0.243, failed-try fees −0.052, account rent back +0.150 → ≈ −0.144; it hit its 100-fill cap ~06:58 PKT, so the last ~9 h were reactive only. Engine end line: fired 152, landed 66, realized +0.704193 (the −0.052 gap to the wallet = pre-send failed-try fees).

## 4b. Pre-send live (pre-placed buys) — separate from the reactive bot
Buys pre-sent after a big whale buy, before any dump. **Not included in sections 5–11**, which cover only the reactive dip bot. Realized = the engine's PRESEND LIVE REALIZED records (chain txs of each buy and sell; the buy pays the token-account rent). Rent back = that rent returned when the engine closed each pre-send account after the exit (dip19+; before dip19 the sell closed it and the refund sat inside realized). Failed tries = fees of tries that landed and failed (6042 price, deadline, …). Net = realized + rent back − failed tries.
